            <!-- image --><div class="td-post-featured-image"><figure><img width="696" height="392" class="entry-thumb lazyload" data-src="https://www.sheppadviser.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/Drone-GVLink-27Augus_48248-696x392.jpg" data-srcset="https://www.sheppadviser.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/Drone-GVLink-27Augus_48248-696x392.jpg 696w, https://www.sheppadviser.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/Drone-GVLink-27Augus_48248-600x338.jpg 600w, https://www.sheppadviser.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/Drone-GVLink-27Augus_48248-740x416.jpg 740w, https://www.sheppadviser.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/Drone-GVLink-27Augus_48248-768x432.jpg 768w, https://www.sheppadviser.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/Drone-GVLink-27Augus_48248-747x420.jpg 747w, https://www.sheppadviser.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/Drone-GVLink-27Augus_48248-1068x601.jpg 1068w, https://www.sheppadviser.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/Drone-GVLink-27Augus_48248-1200x675.jpg 1200w, https://www.sheppadviser.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/Drone-GVLink-27Augus_48248.jpg 1280w" data-sizes="(max-width: 696px) 100vw, 696px" alt="" title="Drone-GVLink-27Augus_48248" src="data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyB3aWR0aD0iMSIgaGVpZ2h0PSIxIiB4bWxucz0iaHR0cDovL3d3dy53My5vcmcvMjAwMC9zdmciPjwvc3ZnPg==" style="--smush-placeholder-width: 696px; --smush-placeholder-aspect-ratio: 696/392;" /><figcaption class="wp-caption-text">GV LINK TAKES SHAPE... After more than two decades of planning, Stage 1 of GV Link Enterprise Park has officially opened, with four large-scale industrial lots now ready to support new investment and business growth in Mooroopna. K&S Pallets and Flavour Makers are among the businesses set to establish operations at the site. Inset: Pictured at the opening of GV Link Enterprise Park Stage 1, from left: Flavour Makers CEO Nick White, Greater Shepparton City Council Mayor Shane Sali, K&S Pallets Managing Director Victor Kyriakou, Greater Shepparton CEO Fiona Le Gassick, and BMD Urban Project Manager Greg Stevens. Photo: Deanne Jeffers</figcaption></figure></div>
            <!-- content --><div class="td-a-rec td-a-rec-id-content_top  tdi_46 td_block_template_1">
<style>.tdi_46.td-a-rec{text-align:center}.tdi_46.td-a-rec:not(.td-a-rec-no-translate){transform:translateZ(0)}.tdi_46 .td-element-style{z-index:-1}.tdi_46.td-a-rec-img{text-align:left}.tdi_46.td-a-rec-img img{margin:0 auto 0 0}.tdi_46 .td_spot_img_all img,.tdi_46 .td_spot_img_tl img,.tdi_46 .td_spot_img_tp img,.tdi_46 .td_spot_img_mob img{border-style:none}@media (max-width:767px){.tdi_46.td-a-rec-img{text-align:center}}</style></div>
<p class="p1 wp-block-paragraph"><span class="s1"><i>By Deanne Jeffers<span class="Apple-converted-space">&nbsp;</span></i></span></p>



<p class="p2 wp-block-paragraph"><span class="s1">AFTER more than two decades of planning, the vision for GV Link Enterprise Park has finally become reality, with the $24M Stage 1 project officially opened on Friday.<span class="Apple-converted-space">&nbsp;</span></span></p>



<p class="p1 wp-block-paragraph"><span class="s1">The first four lots at the 55-hectare Mooroopna site are now ready to accommodate large-scale industrial businesses, unlocking opportunities for expansion and new investment across the region.<span class="Apple-converted-space">&nbsp; &nbsp; &nbsp; &nbsp; &nbsp; &nbsp; &nbsp; &nbsp; &nbsp; &nbsp; &nbsp; &nbsp; &nbsp; &nbsp; &nbsp;&nbsp;</span></span></p>



<p class="p2 wp-block-paragraph"><span class="s2">Among the first businesses to establish at GV Link is K&amp;S Pallets, which will consolidate its two existing facilities into a new purpose-built site.<span class="Apple-converted-space">&nbsp;</span></span></p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-large is-resized"><img fetchpriority="high" decoding="async" width="740" height="416" src="https://www.sheppadviser.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/Drone-GVLink-27Augus_48248-740x416.jpg" alt="" class="wp-image-69829" style="aspect-ratio:1.778912969988356;width:1068px;height:auto" srcset="https://www.sheppadviser.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/Drone-GVLink-27Augus_48248-740x416.jpg 740w, https://www.sheppadviser.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/Drone-GVLink-27Augus_48248-600x338.jpg 600w, https://www.sheppadviser.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/Drone-GVLink-27Augus_48248-768x432.jpg 768w, https://www.sheppadviser.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/Drone-GVLink-27Augus_48248-747x420.jpg 747w, https://www.sheppadviser.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/Drone-GVLink-27Augus_48248-696x392.jpg 696w, https://www.sheppadviser.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/Drone-GVLink-27Augus_48248-1068x601.jpg 1068w, https://www.sheppadviser.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/Drone-GVLink-27Augus_48248-1200x675.jpg 1200w, https://www.sheppadviser.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/Drone-GVLink-27Augus_48248.jpg 1280w" sizes="(max-width: 740px) 100vw, 740px" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">GV LINK TAKES SHAPE&#8230; After more than two decades of planning, Stage 1 of GV Link Enterprise Park has officially opened, with four large-scale industrial lots now ready to support new investment and business growth in Mooroopna. K&amp;S Pallets and Flavour Makers are among the businesses set to establish operations at the site. Photo: Deanne Jeffers</figcaption></figure>



<p class="p1 wp-block-paragraph"><span class="s2">Managing director Victor Kyriakou said committing to the project when the site was still a paddock had been a leap of faith.<span class="Apple-converted-space">&nbsp;</span></span></p>



<p class="p1 wp-block-paragraph"><span class="s2">Perhaps summing it up best when he said at the opening, “Who would have thought this was actually going to happen?”<span class="Apple-converted-space">&nbsp;</span></span></p>



<p class="p1 wp-block-paragraph"><span class="s2">K&amp;S Pallets currently employs about 50 people and handles around 800,000 pallets moving in and out of its facilities each year.<span class="Apple-converted-space">&nbsp;</span></span></p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-large is-resized"><img decoding="async" width="740" height="493" data-src="https://www.sheppadviser.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/P1150642_48202-740x493.jpg" alt="" class="wp-image-69830 lazyload" style="--smush-placeholder-width: 740px; --smush-placeholder-aspect-ratio: 740/493;aspect-ratio:1.5010617730024405;width:1068px;height:auto" data-srcset="https://www.sheppadviser.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/P1150642_48202-740x493.jpg 740w, https://www.sheppadviser.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/P1150642_48202-600x400.jpg 600w, https://www.sheppadviser.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/P1150642_48202-768x512.jpg 768w, https://www.sheppadviser.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/P1150642_48202-630x420.jpg 630w, https://www.sheppadviser.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/P1150642_48202-696x464.jpg 696w, https://www.sheppadviser.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/P1150642_48202-1068x712.jpg 1068w, https://www.sheppadviser.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/P1150642_48202.jpg 1280w" data-sizes="(max-width: 740px) 100vw, 740px" src="data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyB3aWR0aD0iMSIgaGVpZ2h0PSIxIiB4bWxucz0iaHR0cDovL3d3dy53My5vcmcvMjAwMC9zdmciPjwvc3ZnPg==" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">OPEN FOR BUSINESS&#8230; Pictured at the opening of GV Link Enterprise Park Stage 1, from left: Flavour Makers CEO Nick White, Greater Shepparton City Council Mayor Shane Sali, K&amp;S Pallets Managing Director Victor Kyriakou, Greater Shepparton CEO Fiona Le Gassick, and BMD Urban Project Manager Greg Stevens. Photo: Deanne Jeffers</figcaption></figure>



<p class="p1 wp-block-paragraph"><span class="s2">The new facility is expected to create about 10 additional jobs and allow the business to operate around the clock, while providing space to take on larger projects and improve efficiency.<span class="Apple-converted-space">&nbsp;</span></span></p>



<p class="p1 wp-block-paragraph"><span class="s2">“We’ll have a lot more room to expand and be more efficient internally, as well as take on larger projects, which we’ve actually restricted at the moment,” Mr Kyriakou said.<span class="Apple-converted-space">&nbsp;</span></span></p>



<p class="p1 wp-block-paragraph"><span class="s2">Another major investment at GV Link will come from food manufacturer Flavour Makers, which is set to establish a warehouse across two sites.<span class="Apple-converted-space">&nbsp;</span></span></p>



<p class="p1 wp-block-paragraph"><span class="s2">CEO Nick White said the development would position the company for future growth.<span class="Apple-converted-space">&nbsp;</span></span></p>



<p class="p1 wp-block-paragraph"><span class="s2">“With an eye to the future, for the next 50 years, we’ve chosen to build our next-generation food campus right here at GV Link. The Goulburn Valley is the food bowl of Australia &#8230; so effectively, we want to put our next factory in the middle of the orchard,” he said. <span class="Apple-converted-space">&nbsp;</span></span></p>



<p class="p1 wp-block-paragraph"><span class="s2">The scale of GV Link is central to its purpose, with the four Stage 1 lots ranging from nine to 14 hectares.<span class="Apple-converted-space">&nbsp;</span></span></p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-large is-resized"><img decoding="async" width="740" height="493" data-src="https://www.sheppadviser.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/GVLink-August2026-33_48256-740x493.jpg" alt="" class="wp-image-69831 lazyload" style="--smush-placeholder-width: 740px; --smush-placeholder-aspect-ratio: 740/493;aspect-ratio:1.5010617730024405;width:1068px;height:auto" data-srcset="https://www.sheppadviser.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/GVLink-August2026-33_48256-740x493.jpg 740w, https://www.sheppadviser.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/GVLink-August2026-33_48256-600x400.jpg 600w, https://www.sheppadviser.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/GVLink-August2026-33_48256-768x512.jpg 768w, https://www.sheppadviser.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/GVLink-August2026-33_48256-630x420.jpg 630w, https://www.sheppadviser.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/GVLink-August2026-33_48256-696x464.jpg 696w, https://www.sheppadviser.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/GVLink-August2026-33_48256-1068x712.jpg 1068w, https://www.sheppadviser.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/GVLink-August2026-33_48256.jpg 1280w" data-sizes="(max-width: 740px) 100vw, 740px" src="data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyB3aWR0aD0iMSIgaGVpZ2h0PSIxIiB4bWxucz0iaHR0cDovL3d3dy53My5vcmcvMjAwMC9zdmciPjwvc3ZnPg==" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">CELEBRATION&#8230; Stage 1 of GV Link Enterprise Park has officially opened, marking a major milestone for Greater Shepparton after more than 20 years of planning. The 55-hectare development is set to unlock new opportunities for large-scale industrial businesses, investment and jobs across the region. Pictured are representatives from businesses and Greater Shepparton City Council. Photo: Supplied</figcaption></figure>



<p class="p1 wp-block-paragraph"><span class="s2">BMD Urban project manager Greg Stevens said the size of the lots was difficult to conceptualise until the development began taking shape.<span class="Apple-converted-space">&nbsp;</span></span></p>



<p class="p1 wp-block-paragraph"><span class="s2">“Most people are accustomed to seeing a 5,000-square-metre or 10,000-square-metre commercial or industrial lot. We’re talking 14 hectares, 12 hectares, nine hectares,” he said.<span class="Apple-converted-space">&nbsp;</span></span></p>



<p class="p1 wp-block-paragraph"><span class="s2">“It’s really unlocking some new opportunities.”<span class="Apple-converted-space">&nbsp;</span></span></p>



<p class="p1 wp-block-paragraph"><span class="s2">Construction began in April 2025, with works including major earthworks, internal roads, drainage, landscaping and utility connections.<span class="Apple-converted-space">&nbsp;</span></span></p>



<p class="p1 wp-block-paragraph"><span class="s2">BMD delivered extensive underground infrastructure to connect the site with Mooroopna, including about 4.5 kilometres of sewer, two kilometres of water, two kilometres of gas, NBN and power infrastructure.<span class="Apple-converted-space">&nbsp;</span></span></p>



<p class="p1 wp-block-paragraph"><span class="s2">Mr Stevens said the wetland, which is equivalent in volume to Shepparton Lake, was among the final components to be completed.<span class="Apple-converted-space">&nbsp;</span></span></p>



<p class="p1 wp-block-paragraph"><span class="s2">Council has endorsed a pathway forward for Stage 2, with detailed design work to progress as part of the next phase of the development.<span class="Apple-converted-space">&nbsp;</span></span></p>



<p class="p1 wp-block-paragraph"><span class="s2">The project was funded by $9.6M from the Australian Government and $14.4M from Greater Shepparton City Council, with support from the Victorian Government.</span></p>

    <style>

        /* ==================================================
           BASIC ADVERTISEMENT STYLING
           ================================================== */

        .shepp-takeover-ad {

            position: fixed !important;

            top: 200px !important;

            z-index: 5;

            display: block;

        }


        .shepp-takeover-ad a {

            display: block;
            width: 100%;

        }


        .shepp-takeover-ad img {

            display: block !important;

            width: 100% !important;
            height: auto !important;

            max-width: none !important;

        }


        /* ==================================================
           DEFAULT POSITIONS
           ================================================== */

        #shepp-takeover-left {

            left: 0;
            right: auto;

        }


        #shepp-takeover-right {

            right: 0;
            left: auto;

        }


        /* ==================================================
           TABLETS AND PHONES
           ================================================== */

        @media (max-width: 1100px) {

            .shepp-takeover-ad {

                display: none !important;

            }

        }

    </style>


    <script>

    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {

        const leftAd =
            document.getElementById('shepp-takeover-left');

        const rightAd =
            document.getElementById('shepp-takeover-right');


        const maxWidth =
            160;

        const gap =
            5;

        const siteWidth =
            1430;

        const wideBreakpoint =
            2000;


        // ==================================================
        // POSITION TAKEOVER ADS
        // ==================================================

        function positionTakeoverAds() {


            // ----------------------------------------------
            // Hide on small screens
            // ----------------------------------------------

            if (window.innerWidth <= 1100) {

                leftAd.style.display = 'none';
                rightAd.style.display = 'none';

                return;

            }


            // ----------------------------------------------
            // NORMAL DESKTOP VIEW
            //
            // Adviser occupies approximately 84% of
            // viewport, leaving about 8% each side.
            // ----------------------------------------------

            if (window.innerWidth <= wideBreakpoint) {

                const sideSpace =
                    window.innerWidth * 0.08;


                let adWidth =
                    Math.min(
                        maxWidth,
                        sideSpace - gap
                    );


                if (adWidth < 80) {

                    leftAd.style.display = 'none';
                    rightAd.style.display = 'none';

                    return;

                }


                adWidth =
                    Math.floor(adWidth);


                // Set advertisement width

                leftAd.style.width =
                    adWidth + 'px';

                rightAd.style.width =
                    adWidth + 'px';


                // Pin to browser edges

                leftAd.style.left =
                    '0px';

                leftAd.style.right =
                    'auto';


                rightAd.style.right =
                    '0px';

                rightAd.style.left =
                    'auto';

            }


            // ----------------------------------------------
            // VERY WIDE VIEWPORT / REDUCED ZOOM
            //
            // The site has reached its maximum width.
            // Bring the advertisements inward so they
            // remain beside the centred Adviser page.
            // ----------------------------------------------

            else {

                const adWidth =
                    maxWidth;


                const siteLeft =
                    (window.innerWidth - siteWidth) / 2;


                const siteRight =
                    siteLeft + siteWidth;


                // Full-size ads

                leftAd.style.width =
                    adWidth + 'px';

                rightAd.style.width =
                    adWidth + 'px';


                // Left advertisement

                leftAd.style.left =
                    Math.max(
                        0,
                        siteLeft - adWidth - gap
                    ) + 'px';

                leftAd.style.right =
                    'auto';


                // Right advertisement

                rightAd.style.left =
                    (siteRight + gap) + 'px';

                rightAd.style.right =
                    'auto';

            }


            // ----------------------------------------------
            // SHOW ADS
            // ----------------------------------------------

            leftAd.style.display =
                'block';

            rightAd.style.display =
                'block';

        }


        // ==================================================
        // INITIAL POSITION
        // ==================================================

        positionTakeoverAds();


        // ==================================================
        // RECALCULATE AFTER FULL PAGE LOAD
        // ==================================================

        window.addEventListener(
            'load',
            positionTakeoverAds
        );


        // ==================================================
        // RECALCULATE ON WINDOW RESIZE OR ZOOM
        // ==================================================

        window.addEventListener(
            'resize',
            positionTakeoverAds
        );


    });

    </script>
